the pickler memoizes every string it writes\n\n\ndef scan_pickle(data: bytes, location: str) -> list[Finding]:\n    findings: list[Finding] = []\n    recent: list[str] = []  # the last two string pushes, for STACK_GLOBAL\n    try:\n        for op, arg, pos in pickletools.genops(data):\n            if op.name in _STRING_OPS:\n                recent = (recent + [str(arg)])[-2:]\n                for needle in SUSPICIOUS_STRINGS:\n                    if needle in str(arg):\n                        findings.append(Finding(\"code-execution\", f\"{location}@{pos}\", f\"suspicious string {needle!r} in data\"))\n                        break\n                continue\n            if op.name == \"GLOBAL\":\n                module, name = str(arg).split(\" \", 1)\n                _check_global(module, name, f\"{location}@{pos}\", findings)\n            elif op.name == \"STACK_GLOBAL\":\n                if len(recent) == 2:\n                    _check_global(recent[0], recent[1], f\"{location}@{pos}\", findings)\n            elif op.name == \"STOP\":\n                break\n            if op.name not in _STRING_OPS and op.name not in _MEMO_PUT_OPS:\n                recent = []\n    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - truncated or odd pickles are not our problem\n        findings.append(Finding(\"unsupported\", location, f\"could not walk pickle opcodes: {exc}\"))\n    return findings\n\n\ndef _check_global(module: str, name: str, location: str, findings: list[Finding]) -> None:\n    key = f\"{module}.{name}\"\n    kind = PICKLE_BLOCKLIST.get(key)\n    if kind:\n        findings.append(Finding(kind, location, f\"dangerous global {key}\"))\n\"\"\"Keras HDF5 scanning: Lambda layers in the model configuration.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\n\nfrom modelgate.report import Finding\n\n\ndef scan_h5(path: str, location: str) -> list[Finding]:\n    import h5py  # lazy: only needed for Keras files\n\n    with h5py.File(path, \"r\") as fh:\n        raw = fh.attrs.get(\"model_config\")\n    if raw is None:\n        return []\n    if isinstance(raw, bytes):\n        raw = raw.decode(\"utf-8\")\n    config = json.loads(raw)\n    findings: list[Finding] = []\n    for i, layer in enumerate(config.get(\"config\", {}).get(\"layers\", [])):\n        if layer.get(\"class_name\") == \"Lambda\":\n            findings.append(Finding(\"code-execution\", f\"{location}:layers[{i}]\", \"Lambda layer\"))\n    return findings\n\"\"\"TensorFlow SavedModel scanning: parse saved_model.pb and look for file ops.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\n\nfrom modelgate.policy import TF_FILE_OPS\nfrom modelgate.report import Finding\n\n\ndef scan_saved_model_dir(path: str) -> list[Finding]:\n    pb = os.path.join(path, \"saved_model.pb\")\n    if not os.path.isfile(pb):\n        return []\n    from modelgate.tfproto import saved_model_pb2  # {"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_3","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc 'cat /app/docs/threat-model.md /app/docs/report-schema.md /app/evaluate.py'","aggregated_output":"# ModelGate registry policy: what makes an uploaded model artifact malicious\n\nThis is the policy the registry grades scanner verdicts against. It is the\nonly definition that matters; the examples under `/app/samples/` illustrate it\nbut do not enumerate it.\n\n## 1. Policy\n\nAn artifact is **malicious** if, and only if, loading it with its format's\nstandard loader, or invoking a function the artifact exports, would do any of\nthe following as a direct consequence, before the model is otherwise used:\n\n- **(A) code execution**: execute attacker-controlled Python, directly or\n  through indirection. Indirection includes calling a Python callable that\n  evaluates or compiles source, materialising serialized bytecode into a\n  function, and re-entrant deserialization: handing embedded data to another\n  loader that would then do any of (A)-(D). A nested loader is judged by what\n  it would load; a nested loader fed data that does none of (A)-(D) is not a\n  finding.\n- **(B) process**: start a process or run a shell command.\n- **(C) file access**: read, write, create, delete or move a file outside the\n  artifact itself.\n- **(D) network**: open a network connection.\n\nEvery other artifact in scope is **clean**. A false positive and a false\nnegative are equally wrong.\n\nCapabilities are the test, not names. A string that contains `os.system`, a\ndictionary value `\"subprocess\"`, a graph node *named* `ReadFile`, or a constant\ntensor holding `PyFunc` is data, not a finding. Conversely a capability reached\nthrough a harmless-looking path (a memoised reference, a `getattr` chain, a\n`functools.partial` that is retargeted later, a second pickle stream after the\nfirst `STOP`, a function body referenced only through a node attribute) is a\nfinding.\n\nStandard loaders, for the purpose of this policy:\n\n| Format | Loader |\n|---|---|\n| pickle stream(s) | `pickle.load`, called repeatedly until end of file where the format is a sequence of pickles; `joblib.load` |\n| PyTorch checkpoint | `torch.load(weights_only=False)` |\n| NumPy | `numpy.load(allow_pickle=True)` |\n| TensorFlow SavedModel | `tf.saved_model.load` with the `serve` tag set, then every exported signature is called |\n| Keras HDF5 and `.keras` | `keras.models.load_model` with unsafe deserialization enabled (`safe_mode=False`, or `keras.config.enable_unsafe_deserialization()` for HDF5), then the model is called |\n| compressed or archived artifact | the registry unpacks it and loads each member by that member's own format |\n\n## 2. Formats and containers in scope\n\n| Format | Shapes that occur | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| Raw pickle | `.pkl .pickle .bin .joblib`; protocols 0 to 5; one stream, or several concatenated streams read until end of file | protocol-0 text opcodes (`c`, `i`, `o`, `p`, `g`, `R`, `b`) are as valid as their binary forms |\n| PyTorch zip checkpoint | `.pt .pth .bin .ckpt`; a zip holding `<prefix>/data.pkl`, `<prefix>/data/<n>` storages, `<prefix>/version`, `<prefix>/byteorder`; stored entries, a zip64 end-of-central-directory record with 32-bit sizes, as `torch.save` writes it | `torch.load` requires the local-header magic at offset 0 |\n| PyTorch legacy checkpoint | five consecutive pickles (magic, protocol, system info, the object, storage keys) followed by raw storage bytes | the object pickle is the fourth stream |\n| NumPy | `.npy` with object dtype (header, then a pickle); `.npz` (a zip of `.npy` members) | |\n| Compressed / archived | `.gz` (one or several members), `.bz2`, `.xz`, `.zip` (possibly with bytes before the first local file header, as `zipfile` tolerates: the central directory decides, a later entry with the same name wins), `.tar`, `.tar.gz`, `.tgz`, wrapping any in-scope artifact, nested up to three levels | content, not extension, decides the format at every level |\n| TensorFlow SavedModel | a directory holding `saved_model.pb` or `saved_model.pbtxt` and `variables/` | every `meta_graphs[*]` is in scope, and within each: `graph_def.node`, every `graph_def.library.function[*].node_def`, and every function referenced through a node attribute (`If`, `StatelessIf`, `While`, `StatelessWhile`, `Case`, `PartitionedCall`, `StatefulPartitionedCall`, `MapDefun`, `For`) |\n| Keras HDF5 | `.h5 .hdf5`; the root attribute `model_config` holds the model JSON | sub-models nest at any depth; wrapper layers (`TimeDistributed`, `Bidirectional`) hold a layer under `layer` |\n| Keras v3 archive | `.keras`; a zip holding `config.json`, `metadata.json`, `model.weights.h5` | same JSON structure as `model_config` |\n\nOut of scope and never submitted to the registry: ONNX, GGUF, safetensors,\nTorchScript `code/*.py`, TFLite, PMML, XGBoost and LightGBM native formats,\nKeras SavedModel `keras_metadata.pb`, HDF5 external links and virtual\ndatasets, archives with 64-bit sizes or zip64 extra fields.\n\n## 3. Pickle semantics the policy follows\n\nThese are the facts of CPython's unpickler; the policy is defined in terms of\nwhat the loader would actually do.\n\n- `REDUCE` calls the callable with the arguments; on a class that runs\n  `__new__` and `__init__`. `INST` and `OBJ` with arguments construct fully.\n  `BUILD` calls `__setstate__` on the object (for `functools.partial` that\n  retargets the partial). These are the ways a capability executes.\n- `NEWOBJ` and `NEWOBJ_EX`, and argument-less `INST`/`OBJ`, call `__new__`\n  only; they execute no capability. `PERSID`/`BINPERSID` are resolved by the\n  loader's own storage mechanism and execute nothing. Importing a module\n  executes no capability.\n- A global that is only stored as data (placed in a container, bound as an\n  attribute, memoised and never called) is not a finding, whatever its name.\n  A global becomes a finding when the capability it carries is executed, no\n  matter how it reached the executing opcode: directly, through the memo\n  (`GET`, `BINGET`, `LONG_BINGET`), through `builtins.getattr`,\n  `operator.attrgetter`, `operator.methodcaller`, `functools.partial`,\n  `builtins.__import__` or `importlib.import_module`, or in a later stream of\n  the same file.\n- Module aliases are the same module: `posix` and `nt` are `os`, `_pickle` is\n  `pickle`, `_io` is `io`, and every Python 2 name the unpickler maps back\n  with `fix_imports` (the `_compat_pickle` tables): `__builtin__` is\n  `builtins`, `copy_reg` is `copyreg`, `commands` is `subprocess`, `urllib2`\n  is `urllib.request`, `httplib` is `http.client`, and so on. Pickles written\n  below protocol 3 carry those Python 2 names.\n\nRepresentative capability-bearing callables (a seed list, not the policy;\nanything with the same capability counts):\n\n- (A) `builtins.eval`, `builtins.exec`, `builtins.compile`, `marshal.loads`\n  with `types.FunctionType`,\n  `runpy.run_path`, `runpy._run_code`, `timeit.timeit`,\n  `code.InteractiveInterpreter.runsource`, `bdb.Bdb.run`, `pdb.run`,\n  `numpy.testing._private.utils.runstring`, `logging.config.dictConfig`;\n  nested loaders `pickle.loads`, `_pickle.loads`, `torch.load`,\n  `torch.storage._load_from_bytes`, `numpy.load`,\n  `numpy.lib.format.read_array`, `joblib.load`, `pandas.read_pickle`.\n  `builtins.__import__`, `importlib.import_module`, `builtins.getattr`,\n  `operator.attrgetter`, `operator.methodcaller` and `functools.partial`\n  carry no capability of their own; they are the ways a capability-bearing\n  callable is reached, and the call that reaches it is the finding.\n- (B) `os.system`, `os.popen`, `os.execv`, `os.execvp`, `os.execve`,\n  `os.spawnl`, `os.spawnv`, `os.posix_spawn`, `subprocess.Popen`,\n  `subprocess.call`, `subprocess.run`, `subprocess.check_call`,\n  `subprocess.check_output`, `pty.spawn`, `platform.popen`, `pydoc.pipepager`.\n- (C) `builtins.open`, `io.open`, `os.remove`, `os.unlink`, `os.rename`,\n  `os.replace`, `os.mkdir`, `os.makedirs`, `os.rmdir`, `shutil.copy`,\n  `shutil.copyfile`, `shutil.move`, `shutil.rmtree`, `pathlib.Path.write_text`,\n  `pathlib.Path.write_bytes`, `pathlib.Path.unlink`; a nested loader given a\n  path instead of embedded data reads that path.\n- (D) `socket.create_connection`, `socket.socket.connect`,\n  `urllib.request.urlopen`, `http.client.HTTPConnection.connect` and\n  `.request`, `ftplib.FTP.connect` and `ftplib.FTP(host)`,\n  `smtplib.SMTP(host)`, `ssl.get_server_certificate`, `webbrowser.open`. The\n  capability is in the call that connects: constructing a client object\n  without a host (`socket.socket()`, `http.client.HTTPConnection(host, port)`,\n  `ftplib.FTP()`, `xmlrpc.client.ServerProxy(uri)`) opens nothing.\n\n## 4. TensorFlow op table (closed)\n\nFor TensorFlow graphs the table is the policy: an op listed here has the\ncapability listed, and an op not listed has none, wherever it appears.\n\n| Capability | Ops |\n|---|---|\n| (A) code execution | `PyFunc` `PyFuncStateless` `EagerPyFunc` |\n| (C) file access | `ReadFile` `WriteFile` `MatchingFiles` `MatchingFilesDataset` `ImmutableConst` `WholeFileReader` `WholeFileReaderV2` `TextLineReader` `TextLineReaderV2` `FixedLengthRecordReader` `FixedLengthRecordReaderV2` `TFRecordReader` `TFRecordReaderV2` `LMDBReader` `LMDBDataset` `ExperimentalLMDBDataset` `TextLineDataset` `TFRecordDataset` `FixedLengthRecordDataset` `DatasetToTFRecord` `ExperimentalDatasetToTFRecord` `SaveDataset` `SaveDatasetV2` `LoadDataset` `SnapshotDataset` `SnapshotDatasetV2` `LoadAndRemapMatrix` `GenerateVocabRemapping` `Save` `SaveSlices` `Restore` `RestoreSlice` |\n| (D) network | `DataServiceDataset` `DataServiceDatasetV2` `DataServiceDatasetV3` `DataServiceDatasetV4` `RegisterDataset` `RegisterDatasetV2` `DistributedSave` |\n| never a finding | `SaveV2` `RestoreV2` `MergeV2Checkpoints` `ShardedFilename` `ShardedFilespec` (the standard checkpoint saver that every real SavedModel carries), and every op not listed above |\n\nOp names are exact and case-sensitive. Node names, device strings, attribute\nvalues and tensor contents carry no capability.\n\n## 5. Keras\n\nA `Lambda` layer whose `function` carries serialized bytecode is (A):\n`\"function\": {\"class_name\": \"__lambda__\", \"config\": {\"code\": \"<base64>\", \"defaults\": ..., \"closure\": ...}}`. A `Lambda` whose function is a named\nfunction reference (`{\"module\": \"builtins\", \"class_name\": \"function\",\n\"config\": \"relu\", ...}`), an `Activation` layer, and every other layer type are\nclean. Layers are found wherever the JSON nests them: `layers`, the `layer` of\na wrapper, and the `config` of a nested model.\n# ModelGate scanner contract\n\n## Invocation\n\nThe registry runs the scanner once per uploaded artifact, from a working\ndirectory that is not `/app`, with no environment variables beyond `PATH`,\n`HOME` and `TMPDIR`:\n\n```\npython3 -I /app/modelgate/cli.py scan --output <report.json> <path>\n```\n\n`<path>` is the artifact: a file, or a directory for a TensorFlow SavedModel.\nThe scanner must write exactly one JSON document to `<report.json>` and exit\nwith status 0 once it is written. Exit status 2 is reserved for a usage error\n(missing or unknown arguments). Only `/app/modelgate/` is deployed to the\nregistry; the scanner may use nothing outside it except the Python packages\nalready installed in this environment. The registry's held-out corpus holds\nroughly 300 artifacts and is scanned in one pass, one invocation per artifact:\nthe whole pass must finish within 900 seconds and no single artifact may take\nmore than 60 seconds.\n\n## Report\n\n```json\n{\n  \"scanner\": \"modelgate\",\n  \"results\": [\n    {\n      \"path\": \"<the path argument exactly as given>\",\n      \"verdict\": \"malicious\",\n      \"findings\": [\n        {\n          \"kind\": \"code-execution\",\n          \"location\": \"archive/data.pkl\",\n          \"detail\": \"REDUCE builtins.eval\"\n        }\n      ]\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\nRules:\n\n- `results` has exactly one entry per path argument, in argument order, and\n  `path` repeats the argument byte for byte.\n- `verdict` is `\"malicious\"` or `\"clean\"`.\n- `findings` is a list that is non-empty if and only if the verdict is\n  `\"malicious\"`.\n- each finding is an object with exactly the three string keys `kind`,\n  `location` and `detail`.\n- `kind` is one of `\"code-execution\"`, `\"process\"`, `\"file-access\"`,\n  `\"network\"`, `\"unsupported\"`.\n- `location` names where the finding is (a container member, a pickle stream\n  and opcode offset, a graph node, a layer path) and `detail` says what it is.\n  Both are free text for humans.\n\nAn artifact the scanner cannot analyse must still receive a verdict. The\nregistry policy is fail-closed: report it `\"malicious\"` with one finding of\nkind `\"unsupported\"` that says why.\n\nA report that is missing, is not valid JSON, violates any rule above, or is\nnot written within the time limit counts as a wrong verdict for that artifact.\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Run the ModelGate scanner over /app/samples the way the registry does and\ncompare its verdicts with /app/samples/labels.json.\n\nUsage: python3 /app/evaluate.py [--samples DIR] [--scanner /app/modelgate/cli.py]\n\nThe scanner is invoked as a separate process per artifact, with the same\ncommand line the registry uses, so this script never imports the scanner.\n\"\"\"\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nimport time\n\nKINDS = {\"code-execution\", \"process\", \"file-access\", \"network\", \"unsupported\"}\n\n\ndef run_scanner(cli, artifact, timeout=60):\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n        report = os.path.join(tmp, \"report.json\")\n        cmd = [sys.executable, \"-I\", cli, \"scan\", \"--output\", report, artifact]\n        started = time.monotonic()\n        try:\n            proc = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=tmp, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)\n        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n            return None, \"timeout\", time.monotonic() - started\n        seconds = time.monotonic() - started\n        if proc.returncode != 0:\n            return None, f\"exit {proc.returncode}: {proc.stderr.strip()[-200:]}\", seconds\n        try:\n            with open(report, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n                doc = json.load(f)\n        except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:\n            return None, f\"unreadable report: {exc}\", seconds\n        return doc, None, seconds\n\n\ndef check_schema(doc, artifact):\n    if not isinstance(doc, dict) or doc.get(\"scanner\") != \"modelgate\":\n        return \"top-level object with scanner == modelgate expected\"\n    results = doc.get(\"results\")\n    if not isinstance(results, list) or len(results) != 1:\n        return \"results must hold exactly one entry\"\n    entry = results[0]\n    if not isinstance(entry, dict) or set(entry) != {\"path\", \"verdict\", \"findings\"}:\n        return \"entry must have exactly path, verdict, findings\"\n    if entry[\"path\"] != artifact:\n        return \"path must repeat the argument exactly\"\n    if entry[\"verdict\"] not in (\"malicious\", \"clean\"):\n        return \"verdict must be malicious or clean\"\n    findings = entry[\"findings\"]\n    if not isinstance(findings, list):\n        return \"findings must be a list\"\n    if (entry[\"verdict\"] == \"malicious\") != (len(findings) > 0):\n        return \"findings must be non-empty iff verdict is malicious\"\n    for finding in findings:\n        if not isinstance(finding, dict) or set(finding) != {\"kind\", \"location\", \"detail\"}:\n            return \"each finding needs exactly kind, location, detail\"\n        if not all(isinstance(finding[k], str) for k in (\"kind\", \"location\", \"detail\")):\n            return \"finding fields must be strings\"\n        if finding[\"kind\"] not in KINDS:\n            return f\"unknown finding kind {finding['kind']!r}\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--samples\", default=\"/app/samples\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--scanner\", default=\"/app/modelgate/cli.py\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    with open(os.path.join(args.samples, \"labels.json\"), encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        labels = json.load(f)\n\n    rows = []\n    for name in sorted(labels):\n        expected = labels[name]\n        artifact = os.path.join(args.samples, name)\n        doc, error, seconds = run_scanner(args.scanner, artifact)\n        if error is None:\n            error = check_schema(doc, artifact)\n        if error is None:\n            entry = doc[\"results\"][0]\n            verdict = entry[\"verdict\"]\n            kinds = sorted({f[\"kind\"] for f in entry[\"findings\"]})\n        else:\n            verdict = \"invalid\"\n            kinds = []\n        rows.append((name, expected, verdict, kinds, error, seconds))\n\n    width = max(len(r[0]) for r in rows)\n    print(f\"{'artifact':<{width}}  expected   reported   ok  seconds  findings\")\n    for name, expected, verdict, kinds, error, seconds in rows:\n        ok = \"yes\" if verdict == expected else \"NO\"\n        extra = \",\".join(kinds) if error is None else f\"ERROR: {error}\"\n        print(f\"{name:<{width}}  {expected:<9}  {verdict:<9}  {ok:<3} {seconds:7.2f}  {extra}\")\n\n for r in clean if r[2] != \"clean\")\n    invalid = sum(1 for r in rows if r[4] is not None)\n    print()\n    print(f\"recall: {caught}/{len(malicious)} malicious artifacts reported malicious\")\n    print(f\"false positives: {false_pos}/{len(clean)} clean artifacts not reported clean\")\n    print(f\"invalid reports: {invalid}\")\n    print(f\"slowest scan: {max(r[5] for r in rows):.2f} s\")\n __init__(self, location, depth=0):\n        self.location = location\n        self.depth = depth\n        self.findings = []\n        self.memo = {}\n        self.steps = 0\n        self.call_depth = 0\n        self.where = location\n        self.op = ''\n        self.last_value = UNKNOWN\n        self.consumed = 0\n\n    def finding(self, kind, detail):\n        if len(self.findings) < 1000:\n            self.findings.append(Finding(kind, self.where, detail))\n\n    def run(self, data, *, first_only=False):\n        if self.depth > 24:\n            raise AnalysisError('embedded loader nesting limit exceeded')\n        stream = io.BytesIO(data)\n        stream_no = 0\n        legacy = False\n        while stream.tell() < len(data):\n            stack = []\n            protocol = 0\n            stream_no += 1\n            stopped = False\n            for opcode, arg, pos in pickletools.genops(stream):\n                self.steps += 1\n                if self.steps > MAX_STEPS:\n                    raise AnalysisError('pickle opcode limit exceeded')\n                name = opcode.name\n                self.op = name\n                self.where = f'{self.location}:stream[{stream_no}]@{pos}'\n                if name == 'PROTO':\n                    protocol = arg\n                    if arg > 5:\n                        raise AnalysisError(f'unsupported pickle protocol {arg}')\n                elif name == 'FRAME':\n                    pass\n                elif name == 'MARK':\n                    stack.append(MARK)\n                elif name == 'STOP':\n                    if not stack:\n                        raise AnalysisError('STOP on empty stack')\n                    self.last_value = stack[-1]\n                    stopped = True\n                    break\n                elif name == 'POP':\n                    stack.pop()\n                elif name == 'POP_MARK':\n                    self.pop_mark(stack)\n                elif name == 'DUP':\n                    stack.append(stack[-1])\n                elif name in ('NONE', 'NEWTRUE', 'NEWFALSE'):\n                    stack.append({'NONE': None, 'NEWTRUE': True, 'NEWFALSE': False}[name])\n                elif name in ('INT', 'BININT', 'BININT1', 'BININT2', 'LONG', 'LONG1', 'LONG4',\n                              'FLOAT', 'BINFLOAT', 'STRING', 'BINSTRING', 'SHORT_BINSTRING',\n                              'UNICODE', 'BINUNICODE', 'SHORT_BINUNICODE', 'BINUNICODE8',\n                              'BINBYTES', 'SHORT_BINBYTES', 'BINBYTES8', 'BYTEARRAY8'):\n                    stack.append(arg)\n                elif name == 'EMPTY_TUPLE':\n                    stack.append(())\n                elif name == 'EMPTY_LIST':\n                    stack.append([])\n                elif name == 'EMPTY_DICT':\n                    stack.append({})\n                elif name == 'EMPTY_SET':\n                    stack.append(set())\n                elif name in ('TUPLE', 'LIST', 'DICT', 'FROZENSET'):\n                    values = self.pop_mark(stack)\n                    if name == 'TUPLE': value = tuple(values)\n                    elif name == 'LIST': value = values\n                    elif name == 'FROZENSET': value = frozenset(values)\n                    else: value = dict(zip(values[::2], values[1::2]))\n                    stack.append(value)\n                elif name in ('TUPLE1', 'TUPLE2', 'TUPLE3'):\n                    count = int(name[-1])\n                    value = tuple(stack[-count:])\n                    del stack[-count:]\n                    stack.append(value)\n                elif name == 'APPEND':\n                    value = stack.pop()\n                    self.append(stack[-1], [value])\n                elif name in ('APPENDS', 'ADDITEMS'):\n                    values = self.pop_mark(stack)\n                    if name == 'ADDITEMS' and isinstance(stack[-1], set):\n                        stack[-1].update(values)\n                    else:\n                        self.append(stack[-1], values)\n                elif name == 'SETITEM':\n                    value, key = stack.pop(), stack.pop()\n                    self.setitem(stack[-1], key, value)\n                elif name == 'SETITEMS':\n                    values = self.pop_mark(stack)\n                    for k, v in zip(values[::2], values[1::2]):\n                        self.setitem(stack[-1], k, v)\n                elif name in ('PUT', 'BINPUT', 'LONG_BINPUT'):\n                    self.memo[arg] = stack[-1]\n                elif name == 'MEMOIZE':\n                    self.memo[len(self.memo)] = stack[-1]\n                elif name in ('GET', 'BINGET', 'LONG_BINGET'):\n                    if arg not in self.memo:\n                        raise AnalysisError(f'missing pickle memo {arg}')\n                    stack.append(self.memo[arg])\n                elif name in ('GLOBAL', 'INST'):\n                    module, attr = arg.split(' ', 1)\n                    value = global_symbol(module, attr, protocol)\n                    if name == 'INST':\n                        args = tuple(self.pop_mark(stack))\n                        value = self.call(value, args) if args else self.new(value, args)\n                    stack.append(value)\n                elif name == 'STACK_GLOBAL':\n                    attr, module = stack.pop(), stack.pop()\n                    stack.append(global_symbol(module, attr, protocol))\n                elif name == 'REDUCE':\n                    args, fn = stack.pop(), stack.pop()\n                    stack.append(self.call(fn, seq(args)))\n                elif name == 'OBJ':\n                    values = self.pop_mark(stack)\n                    fn, args = values[0], tuple(values[1:])\n                    stack.append(self.call(fn, args) if args else self.new(fn, args))\n                elif name in ('NEWOBJ', 'NEWOBJ_EX'):\n                    kwargs = stack.pop() if name == 'NEWOBJ_EX' else {}\n                    args, cls = stack.pop(), stack.pop()\n                    stack.append(self.new(cls, seq(args), kwargs))\n                elif name == 'BUILD':\n                    state = stack.pop()\n                    self.build(stack[-1], state)\n                    self.joblib_payload(stack[-1], stream)\n                elif name == 'PERSID':\n                    stack.append(Symbol('persistent', value=arg))\n                elif name == 'BINPERSID':\n                    stack.append(Symbol('persistent', value=stack.pop()))\n                elif name == 'NEXT_BUFFER':\n                    stack.append(Symbol('buffer'))\n                elif name == 'READONLY_BUFFER':\n                    pass\n                elif name in ('EXT1', 'EXT2', 'EXT4'):\n                    # Resolving an extension requires a loader-specific registry.\n                    stack.append(Symbol('extension', str(arg)))\n                else:\n                    raise AnalysisError(f'unsupported opcode {name}')\n            if not stopped:\n                raise AnalysisError('pickle has no STOP')\n            self.consumed = stream.tell()\n            if stream_no == 1 and type(self.last_value) is int and self.last_value == TORCH_MAGIC:\n                legacy = True\n            if first_only or (legacy and stream_no == 5):\n                break\n        if not stream_no:\n            raise AnalysisError('empty pickle')\n        return self.last_value\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def pop_mark(stack):\n        for i in range(len(stack) - 1, -1, -1):\n            if stack[i] is MARK:\n                result = stack[i + 1:]\n                del stack[i:]\n                return result\n        raise AnalysisError('pickle MARK missing')\n\n    def append(self, obj, values):\n        if isinstance(obj, list):\n            obj.extend(values)\n        elif isinstance(obj, Symbol):\n            obj.attrs.setdefault('_items', []).extend(values)\n        else:\n            raise AnalysisError('APPEND target is not a sequence')\n\n    def setitem(self, obj, key, value):\n        if isinstance(obj, dict):\n            obj[key] = value\n        elif isinstance(obj, (list, bytearray)):\n            obj[key] = value\n        elif isinstance(obj, Symbol):\n            obj.attrs.setdefault('_items', {})[key] = value\n        else:\n            raise AnalysisError('SETITEM target is not a mapping')\n\n    def new(self, cls, args, kwargs=None):\n        # Policy explicitly gives NEWOBJ/NEWOBJ_EX no capability. Preserve\n        # enough __new__ state to understand later BUILD or method calls.\n        if not isinstance(cls, Symbol):\n            return Symbol('instance', args=args)\n        if cls.name == 'functools.partial':\n            return Symbol('partial', args=args[1:], value=args[0] if args else UNKNOWN,\n                          attrs={'keywords': dict(kwargs or {})})\n        return Symbol('instance', cls.name, args=args)\n\n    def build(self, obj, state):\n        if not isinstance(obj, Symbol):\n            return\n        if obj.kind == 'partial' or obj.name == 'functools.partial':\n            if isinstance(state, tuple) and len(state) == 4:\n                obj.kind = 'partial'\n                obj.value, args, keywords, namespace = state\n                obj.args = seq(args)\n                obj.attrs = dict(namespace or {})\n                obj.attrs['keywords'] = dict(keywords or {})\n                return\n            raise AnalysisError('invalid partial state')\n        if obj.name in ('io.BytesIO', 'io.StringIO'):\n            if isinstance(state, tuple) and state:\n                obj.value = state[0]\n                obj.attrs['position'] = state[1] if len(state) > 1 else 0\n        # Some pickles install __setstate__ on an instance before BUILD.\n        setter = obj.attrs.get('__setstate__')\n        if setter is not None:\n            self.call(setter, (state,))\n            return\n        obj.attrs['_state'] = state\n        if isinstance(state, dict):\n            obj.attrs.update(state)\n        elif isinstance(state, tuple) and len(state) == 2:\n            for part in state:\n                if isinstance(part, dict):\n                    obj.attrs.update(part)\n\n    def attr(self, obj, name, default=UNKNOWN):\n        if not isinstance(name, str):\n            return UNKNOWN\n        if isinstance(obj, Symbol):\n            if name in obj.attrs:\n                return obj.attrs[name]\n            if obj.kind == 'partial':\n                if name == 'func': return obj.value\n                if name == 'args': return obj.args\n                if name == 'keywords': return obj.attrs.get('keywords', {})\n            if name == '__dict__':\n                if obj.kind in ('module', 'global'):\n                    return Symbol('namespace', obj.name)\n                return obj.attrs\n            if name in ('__call__',) and obj.kind in ('global', 'partial', 'attrgetter', 'methodcaller', 'itemgetter', 'function'):\n                return obj\n            if obj.kind in ('global', 'module'):\n                return Symbol('global', canonical(obj.name + '.' + name))\n            if obj.kind == 'instance':\n                if name == '__class__':\n                    return Symbol('global', obj.name)\n                return Symbol('bound', canonical(obj.name + '.' + name), value=obj)\n            if obj.kind == 'partial' and name == '__setstate__':\n                return Symbol('bound', 'functools.partial.__setstate__', value=obj)\n            return default\n        if name == '__class__':\n            return Symbol('global', typename(obj))\n        return Symbol('bound',               raise AnalysisError('symbolic call recursion limit exceeded')\n            return self._call(fn, args, kwargs or {})\n        finally:\n            self.call_depth -= 1\n\n    def _call(self, fn, args, kwargs):\n        if not isinstance(fn, Symbol):\n            raise AnalysisError('unresolved callable')\n        if fn.kind == 'partial':\n            keywords = dict(fn.attrs.get('keywords', {}))\n            keywords.update(kwargs)\n            return self.call(fn.value, fn.args + args, keywords)\n        if fn.kind == 'attrgetter':\n            values = []\n            for name in fn.args:\n                obj = args[0]\n                for part in name.split('.'):\n                    obj = self.attr(obj, part)\n                values.append(obj)\n            return values[0] if len(values) == 1 else tuple(values)\n        if fn.kind == 'itemgetter':\n            values = tuple(self.getitem(args[0], key) for key in fn.args)\n            return values[0] if len(values) == 1 else values\n        if fn.kind == 'methodcaller':\n            return self.call(self.attr(args[0], fn.name), fn.args, fn.attrs)\n        if fn.kind == 'function':\n            self.finding('code-execution', f'{self.op} serialized Python function')\n            return UNKNOWN\n        if fn.kind == 'method':\n            return self.call(fn.value, fn.args + args, kwargs)\n        if fn.kind == 'instance':\n            return self.call(self.attr(fn, '__call__'), args, kwargs)\n        if fn.kind not in ('global', 'bound'):\n            raise AnalysisError(f'unresolved callable ({fn.kind})')\n        name = canonical(fn.name)\n        bound = fn.value if fn.kind == 'bound' else None\n        if name.endswith('.__call__') and bound is None:\n            return self.call(Symbol('global', name[:-9]), args, kwargs)\n        if name in ('builtins.getattr', 'builtins.object.__getattribute__', 'builtins.type.__getattribute__', 'operator.getattr'):\n            return self.attr(args[0], args[1], args[2] if len(args) > 2 else UNKNOWN)\n        if name in ('builtins.setattr', 'builtins.object.__setattr__'):\n            if      args[0].attrs.pop(args[1], None)\n            return None\n        if name in ('builtins.__import__', 'importlib.import_module'):\n            module = args[0] if args else kwargs.get('name')\n            if not isinstance(module, str):\n                return UNKNOWN\n            if name == 'builtins.__import__':\n                fromlist = args[3] if len(args) > 3 else kwargs.get('fromlist', ())\n                if not fromlist:\n                    module = module.split('.')[0]\n            return Symbol('module', canonical(module))\n        if name in ('operator.attrgetter', 'functools.partial.__setstate__':\n            obj, state = (bound, args[0]) if bound is not None else args[:2]\n            self.build(obj, state)\n            return None\n        if name == 'types.MethodType':\n            return Symbol('method', value=args[0], args=(args[1],))\n        if name in ('builtins.staticmethod', 'builtins.classmethod'):\n            return Symbol('instance',   return {'__builtins__': Symbol('module', 'builtins')}\n        if name in NESTED_LOADERS:\n            return self.nested(name, args, kwargs, bound)\n        if name in ('pickle.Unpickler',            config = args[0] if args else (bound.args[0] if bound is not None and bound.args else {})\n            self.logging_config(config)\n            return None\n        if name in if name in HOST_CLIENTS:\n            host = args[0] if args else kwargs.get('host', '')\n            if host or name.startswith(('imaplib.', 'poplib.')):\n                self.finding('network', nested(self, name, args, kwargs, bound):\n        from modelgate import dispatch\n\n        if bound is not None and name.startswith('pickle.'):\n            source = bound.value\n        else:\n            source = args[0] if args else next((kwargs[k] for k in ('file', 'f', 'filepath_or_buffer', 'filename', 'data') if k in kwargs), UNKNOWN)\n        byte_loader = name.endswith('.loads') or name == 'torch.storage._load_from_bytes'\n        if self.is_buffer(source):\n            data = source.value\n            position = source.attrs.get('position', 0)\n            if not isinstance(data, (bytes, bytearray, str)):\n                raise AnalysisError(f'unknown buffer for {name}')\n            data = data[position:]\n            if isinstance(data, str):\n                data = data.encode('latin1')\n        elif byte_loader and isinstance(source, (bytes, bytearray)):\n            data = bytes(source)\n        elif isinstance(source, (str, bytes)) or (isinstance(source, Symbol) and source.name.startswith('pathlib.')):\n            self.finding('file-access', f'{self.op} {name} reads a path')\n            return UNKNOWN\n        else:\n            raise AnalysisError(f'cannot statically resolve input to {name}')\n        location = self.where + '->' + name\n        if name.startswith(('pickle.', 'dill.', 'cloudpickle.')):\n            nested = Interpreter(location, self.depth + 1)\n            try:\n                result = nested.run(data, first_only=True)\n            finally:\n                self.findings.extend(nested.findings)\n            if self.is_buffer(source):\n                source.attrs['position'] = position + nested.consumed\n            return result\n        # numpy.load/torch.load/joblib.load consume containers as      for section in ('formatters', 'filters', 'handlers'):\n            entries = config.get(section, {})\n            if not isinstance(entries, dict):\n                continue\n            for spec               kwargs = {k: v for k, v in spec.items() if k not in ('()', 'class', 'level', 'formatter', 'filters', '.')}\n                self.call(factory, (), kwargs)\n\n    def pure_call(self,        if self.is_buffer(bound):\n                value = bound.value\n                pos = bound.attrs.get('position', 0)\n                if method in ('getvalue', 'getbuffer'):\n Numeric/structured numeric payloads are never deserialized as Python.\n    return []\n\n\ndef scan_zip(data, location, *, depth=0):\n    findings = []\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(data)) as zf:\n        infos = zf.infolist()\n        if len(infos) > MAX_MEMBERS:\n            raise ValueError('archive member limit exceeded')\n        # Do not scan a shadowed member or trust local-header names.\n        entries = {info.filename: info for info in infos if not info.is_dir()}\n        if sum(info.file_size for info in entries.values()) torch = data.startswith(b'PK\\\\x03\\\\x04') and any(\n            n[:-len('data.pkl')] + 'version' in entries for n in torch_pickles)\n        if torch:\n            for name in torch_pickles:\n
